## Sheet home
"hey there, Cruithne (s4 Sabrine/OT Terry/s5 ???) here"
"this spreadsheet was likely posted in dev server(s) by Versil because I'm not there (avoiding spoilers), but feel free to contact me about it if needed"

"so basically, what I'm doing here, is categorising every* item in the game by what essence types I feel should apply to it."
"*with a few exceptions, listed in Dev Notes tab"
"of course, a lot of this is my own personal viewpoint; as such, I've documented my reasoning (most of it falls into about a hundred (including variations) common groups, listed in Common Reasoning)"
"suggestions/requests are absolutely appreciated, just let me know (through DMing or otherwise) and I'll update it for you; including but not limited to the below:"
- - - - - - - - any essence types that should be added or removed from a particular item
"- - - - - - - - any corrections, for example: me mixing up an item summoning vs an item transforming"
- - - - - - - - any british spellings or typos in item names and such messing with searching
- - - - - - - - giving me (a) homebrew item(s) you want to be made craftable so I can assign essences
please don't worry about giving me a lot of things to add. I'm pretty good at manually updating datasets

"some items (particularly some wondrous items) are a lot more of a stretch than others. I've assigned at least one essence to every item, but some of them were Difficult"
"this does not account for the potential of applying weapon enhancements to new weapons (for example making a Rapier of Fish Command), if this potential is taken into account some finesse weapons may get an extra essence"

my edited version of the original essence type guide can be found at Types of Essence sheet.
"it's in a more readable (for me, anyway) form now, and also has some additions and changes:"
"-a few more reasonings have been added, notably including one for every vanilla warlock pact that wasn't already covered"
"-a few reasonings have been merged together, for example 'spells associated with x damage', 'evocation spells that deal x damage' have just been merged into 'damage: x'"
-lists for items which could have different essences based on the version of the item have been made so as not to have (for example) a lot of entries for armor of resistance when it could just have a note of variable element

"Even if individual essence types aren't used, feel free to use this to search for appropriate loot!"

## Dev Notes
,How the spreadsheet is set up / different sheets
Columns,A guide to the columns in Item List
Item List,Every item in the game that fulfills the specifications
Common Reasoning,"99% of essence-worthy item properties fall into the groups listed here with name, reasoning, and essence type"
Item Reasoning,"The reasoning(s) for every item/essence type combination in Item List, using Common Reasoning groups"
Spell Reasoning,"The reasoning for every spell that is called by an item in Item List, so that it can be crossreferenced to be consistent"
Types of Essence,"The original guide to essence types, in a more readable (for me) format, with additions and lists for things which could use multiple types"
Dev Notes,"you're reading it. could use a better name, it's any lists I'm not sure where else to put"

,Excluded items
High Rarity,"Radar specified to me a while back that the maximum craftable rarity should be Very Rare, so no item above that is on here"
Useless to PCs.,"This includes items PCs can't use, as well as strictly-negative items that require attunement and as such can't be just given to an enemy in a fight."
Band of Loyalty,"die instantly on 0 hp, if someone wants to die that badly they can arrange something rather than craft"
Master's Amulet,"useless if no shield guardian exists, and an amulet will likely exist or be creatable otherwise if needed"
Mind Blade,only works for mind flayers or their thralls
Mind Carapace Armor,only works for mind flayers or their thralls
Mind Lash,only works for mind flayers
Navigation Orb,"useless if no cloud castle exists, and I doubt anyone's getting access to a cloud castle"
Shield Guardian Amulet,"useless if no shield guardian exists, and an amulet will likely exist or be creatable otherwise if needed"
Shield of Far Sight,only useful for mind flayers
Module Specific,"and has no properties that would be useful outside said module, even if module-specific conditions were ignored. anything kept with module-specific conditions has a module override in the Notes column"
Horned Ring,ignores the restrictions of a specific area
Professor Skant,specific version of Professor Orb
Saint Markovia's Thighbone,"fragile mace of disruption with no additional benefit (+ it's sort of weird to just craft someone's thighbone. what is this, relics?)"
Stonky's ring,ring of telekinesis with very specific control
Winter's Dark Bite,no better than an ordinary +1 greatsword except in very specific circumstances
Redundancy
Duplicate items,"generic versions of items, for example Marvelous Pigments vs Nolzur's Marvelous Pigments - the named version is kept for ease of searching"
"Mithral Half Plate, +1",specific item with two properties that can already be stacked
Piwafwi (Cloak of Elvenkind),"strictly inferior to an ordinary cloak of elvenkind, or to the +fire resistance piwafwi"
Specific plus weapons,"such as Boomerang +1, as this is covered by Weapon +1 already"
Stone of Ill Luck,strictly inferior to an ordinary one
Sword of Vengeance,strictly inferior to an ordinary +1 sword
"Vicious Rapier, +1",specific item with two properties that can already be stacked
Unreasonable to make,by my reasoning; if DMs decide they're fine with people making these then that's on them and I'll add essences for them - this includes things that might cause DMs issues if not planned for
Blod Stone,being able to decide to just track down a whole family without it being planned for might cause problems
Devastation Orb,major effects on a wide area
Efreeti Bottle,potential to cast Wish
Mirror of Life Trapping,"just casually trap an NPC (or PC!) and mess up people's plotlines, sure, seems legit"
Mystery Key,sure have a 1/20 chance of skipping any need to search for a key
Other
Manual of Golems,"shortest golem to make takes 30 days, no one is getting that done within game time without planning around a timeskip"
Spell Scrolls,at Radar's request; they have a canonical means of creation already
Spell Gems,"they're basically spell scrolls, there's other means of spell storage"
Spellwrought Tattoos,they're basically spell scrolls
Permanent Stat Boosters,"By request. I forget whose, so if these should be craftable let me know and I'll add them."

## Columns
Name,Item's name. Links to page on Beyond.,🧪 Acidic,🌈 Chaotic,🧊 Cold,🪞 Dimensional,👿 Fiendish,,,Item abbreviations:
Rarity,Item's rarity.,🔥 Fiery,💪 Forceful,⛰️ Inert,⚡ Lightning,🌊 Mercurial,,FoWP,Figurine of Wondrous Power
Category,Item's category.,💀 Necrotic,🦋 Phantasmal,🐍 Poisonous,👁️ Psychic,☀️ Radiant,,IotB,Instrument of the Bards
Source,Item's sourcebook.,🪄 Theurgic,🔊 Thundering,💖 Vital,⚫ Void,🌪️ Zephyrous
Base,Item's base price as given to me by Radar.
SMIP,Item's price in Sane Magical Item Prices (if applicable)
DSCIP,"Item's price in Dungeonsports Coliseum (if applicable, linked)"
Price,Item's decided price in essence.
Emoji rows,"Denote the essence type. Alphabetical, hover for name, or see up-right:"
,"Anything typed in a cell turns it green, indicating the essence is applicable."
Variable,"Any variable essence type for an item, for example using a given element."
Notes,"Any further notes, including suggestions for overriding module-specific elements."


Sourcebook,Abbreviated forms used.
,Note this uses Beyond's item page so doesn't take rereleases into account.
,"If you want a column for 'rereleased in x book(s)', I can go through and check."

## Common Reasoning
Property,Essence type,Reasoning,"if directed here from Spell Reasoning, replace 'item' with 'spell'"
Ability (Charisma),psychic,"item grants bonus or advantage to Charisma stat, checks, saves, and/or skills"
Ability (Constitution),vital,"item grants bonus or advantage to Constitution stat, checks, saves, and/or skills"
Ability (Dexterity),mercurial,"item grants bonus or advantage to Dexterity stat, checks, saves, and/or skills"
Ability (Intelligence),theurgic,"item grants bonus or advantage to Intelligence stat, checks, saves, and/or skills"
Ability (Strength),forceful,"item grants bonus or advantage to Strength stat, checks, saves, and/or skills"
Ability (Wisdom),phantasmal,"item grants bonus or advantage to Wisdom stat, checks, saves, and/or skills"
Aids Spellcasters,theurgic,"item is a spellbook, a spellcasting focus of some kind, increases spell attack rolls or spell save DC, stores spells for later use, or otherwise Aids Spellcasters"
Animated Object,theurgic,item is an animated object
Antimagic,inert,"item resists or nullifies magic, or grants protection from magic"
Awareness,phantasmal,item grants awareness of some kind
Boosts HP,inert,resilience (item grants increased or temporary HP)
Boosts HP,vital,item grants increased or temporary HP
Breathing,zephyrous,"item relates to breathing in some way, such as allowing normal breathing or granting a breath weapon"
Charged Item,theurgic,item has charges to cast spells
Creature (Aberration),void,"item helps, hinders, or otherwise relates to aberrations"
Creature (Celestial),radiant,"item helps, hinders, or otherwise relates to celestials"
Creature (Construct),theurgic,"item helps, hinders, or otherwise relates to constructs"
"Creature (Elemental, Air)",zephyrous,"item helps, hinders, or otherwise relates to air elementals"
"Creature (Elemental, Earth)",inert,"item helps, hinders, or otherwise relates to earth elementals"
"Creature (Elemental, Fire)",fiery,"item helps, hinders, or otherwise relates to fire elementals"
"Creature (Elemental, Water)",mercurial,"item helps, hinders, or otherwise relates to water elementals"
Creature (Fey),phantasmal,"item helps, hinders, or otherwise relates to fey"
Creature (Fiend),fiendish,"item helps, hinders, or otherwise relates to fiends"
Creature (Giant),inert,"item helps, hinders, or otherwise relates to giants"
Creature (Undead),necrotic,"item helps, hinders, or otherwise relates to undead"
Cursed Item,void,item is cursed
Damage (Acid),acidic,item deals acid damage
Damage (Cold),cold,item deals cold damage
Damage (Fire),fiery,item deals fire damage
Damage (Force),forceful,item deals force damage
Damage (Lightning),lightning,item deals lightning damage
Damage (Necrotic),necrotic,item deals necrotic damage
Damage (Poison),poisonous,item deals poison damage
Damage (Psychic),psychic,item deals psychic damage
Damage (Radiant),radiant,item deals radiant damage
Damage (Thunder),thundering,item deals thunder damage
Darkness,void,"causes or has special properties relating to dim light and/or darkness, including darkvision"
Destruction,void,"item can destroy objects and/or creatures asides from just straight-up killing/breaking them: removing limbs, disallowing repair/revival/healing magic, etc"
Dice Manipulation,chaotic,"item allows the user to grant advantage, disadvantage, bonuses, penalties, choice of two rolls etc. to rolls"
Element (Air),zephyrous,item is related to air in some way
Element (Earth),inert,item is related to earth in some way
Element (Fire),fiery,item is related to fire in some way
Element (Water),mercurial,item is related to water in some way
Enhancement (Defensive),inert,"item grants base or bonus AC, a bonus to saves, crit prevention, resistance to at least one standard weapon damage, reduced damage etc."
Enhancement (Offensive),void,"destruction; item gives a weapon increased damage, magical status, higher critical chance etc."
Extradimensional,dimensional,item is fully or partially an extradimensional space
Extraplanar,dimensional,"item is from, relates to, or allows travel to another plane"
Flavour,(Variable),"a purely flavour reason, see item's Details entry"
Floating/Hovering,zephyrous,"item floats, hovers, or flies (without allowing the user to fly)"
Force Field,forceful,item creates a force field
Free Action,mercurial,"fluidity (item prevents reduction of speed by magic or difficult terrain, or (helps) prevents/cures paralysis, grapples, restraint etc)"
Gas,zephyrous,item relates to or creates gas such as clouds and smoke
Healing,vital,"item restores HP, cures conditions or disease, or stabilises or revives a creature"
Immovability,inert,"item causes or allows the user to cause restraint, grapples, paralysis, petrification, stunning, or incapacitation, or prevents the user, itself, or another thing from being moved"
Invisibility,phantasmal,item can become invisible or allow the user to become or see invisible
Knowledge,theurgic,"item grants knowledge of some kind, for example a language or proficiency"
Light,radiant,item emits light
Madness,void,"item causes, cures, or grants resistance or immunity to effects such as madness, berserk, and fear"
Multi (Creature Type),(Creature),essence type may change depending on what creature type(s) the item is chosen to affect
Multi (Deity),(Deity),essence type may change depending on maker's deity
Multi (Element),(Element),essence type may change depending on item's chosen element(s) and/or damage type(s)
Multi (Warlock),(Warlock),essence type may change depending on maker's patron
Obscures Vision,void,darkness; item lightly or heavily obscures vision
Other,(Variable),"a property too uncommon to be listed here, see Details for reasoning"
Plane (Far Realm),void,item is from or related to the Far Realm
Plane (Feywild),phantasmal,item is from or related to the Feywild
Plane (Lower),fiendish,item is from or related to one of the Lower Planes
Plane (Shadowfell),void,item is from or related to the Shadowfell
Plane (Upper),radiant,item is from or related to one of the Upper Planes
Poison,poisonous,item causes or cures poisoned condition
Pushing/Pulling/Shoving,forceful,"item allows the user to push, pull, or shove creatures or objects, including knocking them prone"
Resistance (Acid),acidic,"item grants vulnerability, resistance, or immunity to acid damage"
Resistance (Cold),cold,"item grants vulnerability, resistance, or immunity to cold damage"
Resistance (Fire),fiery,"item grants vulnerability, resistance, or immunity to fire damage"
Resistance (Force),forceful,"item grants vulnerability, resistance, or immunity to force damage"
Resistance (Lightning),lightning,"item grants vulnerability, resistance, or immunity to lightning damage"
Resistance (Necrotic),necrotic,"item grants vulnerability, resistance, or immunity to necrotic damage"
Resistance (Poison),poisonous,"item grants vulnerability, resistance, or immunity to poison damage"
Resistance (Psychic),psychic,"item grants vulnerability, resistance, or immunity to psychic damage"
Resistance (Radiant),radiant,"item grants vulnerability, resistance, or immunity to radiant damage"
Resistance (Thunder),thundering,"item grants vulnerability, resistance, or immunity to thunder damage"
School (Abjuration),forceful,item is related to Abjuration
School (Conjuration),dimensional,"item is related to Conjuration, including items explicitly summoning a creature or object"
School (Divination),theurgic,item is related to Divination
School (Enchantment),psychic,item is related to Enchantment
School (Illusion),phantasmal,item is related to Illusion
School (Necromancy),necrotic,item is related to Necromancy
School (Transmutation),mercurial,item is related to Transmutation
Senses,phantasmal,"item boosts senses in some way, such as granting them, allowing using another's senses, or curing blindness/deafness"
Sound,thundering,item emits notable sound
Spell,(Variable),"item can cast or emulate a spell, see Spell Reasoning for the spells given in Details"
Telekinesis,forceful,"item allows the user to push, pull, or otherwise manipulate things with their mind"
Telekinesis,psychic,"item allows the user to push, pull, or otherwise manipulate things with their mind"
Telepathy,psychic,"item grants or shields against mind reading, including communication"
Teleportation,dimensional,item allows the user to teleport
Transformation,mercurial,the item causes or ends some form of transformation in either itself or another creature or object
Travel (Burrow),inert,item grants or increases burrow speed
Travel (Climb),forceful,"item grants or increases climbing speed, or otherwise aids in climbing"
Travel (Flight),zephyrous,"item grants or increases flight speed, or otherwise allows flight"
Travel (Swim),mercurial,"item grants or increases swimming speed, or otherwise aids in swimming"
Travel (Walk),mercurial,"item increases walking speed (reasoning for mercurial: transmutation, see: Haste/Longstrider)"
Underwater,mercurial,has special properties underwater
Unpredictability,chaotic,item has a random effect or is otherwise unpredictable. any essence relating only to a random potential is not valid
Weapon (Dexterity),mercurial,"ranged or finesse melee weapon, uses dexterity"
Weapon (Finesse),(Finesse),"weapon can be one of multiple melee OR ranged types, some but not all of which are finesse"
Weapon (Strength),forceful,"melee or finesse ranged weapon, uses strength"
Weapon (Variable),(Weapon),weapon can be melee or ranged

Variable essence types:,,"(this is currently just speculation on Crui's part, note some of these are also italicised in the above meaning they're ones I'm suggesting to add)"
(Warlock),Anything marked with (Warlock) can use the appropriate essence type to the maker's patron:
,Archfey,Phantasmal
,Celestial,Radiant
,Fathomless,Mercurial
,Fiend,Fiendish
,Genie,Dimensional,(as well as the four subtypes - these are easy ones to assign)
,Genie (dao),Inert
,Genie (djinni),Zephyrous
,Genie (efreeti),Fiery
,Genie (marid),Mercurial
,Great Old One,Void
,Hexblade,Psychic,"(this one is a bit of a stretch, I admit; hex is an enchantment is my logic)"
,Undead,Necrotic
,Undying,Vital,(necrotic could also work here in theory)
(Weapon),"This item could be a melee or a ranged weapon, and so can use the appropriate essence."
,Melee weapons,Forceful
,Ranged weapons,Mercurial
,Finesse weapons,Forceful or Mercurial
(Finesse),"While this item can only be melee OR ranged, some options are finesse weapons, allowing these options to use both forceful and mercurial instead of one or the other depending on range."
(Element),"This item can be related to multiple elements and/or damage types, the appropriate essence(s) can be used."
,Acid,Acidic
,Air,Zephyrous
,Cold,Cold
,Earth,Inert
,Fire,Fiery
,Force,Forceful
,Lightning,Lightning
,Necrotic,Necrotic
,Poison,Poisonous
,Psychic,Psychic
,Radiant,Radiant
,Thunder,Thundering
,Water,Mercurial
(Creature),"This item can be related to multiple creature types, the appropriate essence(s) can be used."
,Aberrations,Void
,Celestials,Radiant
,Constructs,Theurgic
,Elementals,"Fiery, Inert, Mercurial, Zephyrous"
,Fey,Phantasmal
,Fiends,Fiendish
,Giants,Forceful
,Undead,Necrotic
(Deity),"Anything marked with (Deity) could use an appropriate essence type to the maker's deity, if deemed reasonable on a case-by-case basis."
,"I'm listing some cleric domains as a baseline the same way as warlock pacts, but not all domains can easily be matched with an essence, and not everyone using (Deity) would be a cleric."
,Cleric: Arcana,Theurgic
,Cleric: Death,"Necrotic, Void"
,Cleric: Forge,"Fiery, Inert"
,Cleric: Grave,Necrotic
,Cleric: Knowledge,Theurgic
,Cleric: Life,Vital
,Cleric: Light,Radiant
,Cleric: Tempest,"Lightning, Mercurial, Thundering"
,Cleric: Trickery,Phantasmal
,Cleric: Twilight,"Radiant, Void"
